Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on macroevolution structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 66. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 47 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on macroevolution structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 66. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 47. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in macroevolution theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 987 and the invariant I(A,B) = 209 mod 3.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to macroevolution theory, we decompose the problem into 987 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 209. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
